    SELECT TOP(1) [u].[user_id], [u].[created_date], [u].[email], [u].[full_name], [u].[is_active], [u].[password_hash], [u].[phone_number], [u].[role_id], [u].[two_factor_email], [u].[two_factor_enabled], [r].[role_id] AS [role_id0], [r].[description], [r].[role_name]

    SELECT TOP(1) [u].[user_id], [u].[created_date], [u].[email], [u].[full_name], [u].[is_active], [u].[password_hash], [u].[phone_number], [u].[role_id], [u].[two_factor_email], [u].[two_factor_enabled], [r].[role_id] AS [role_id0], [r].[description], [r].[role_name]
